- **mm-proveout-red-team**: Dispatch mm-proveout-red-team during /polytropos:execute mm-proveout after the verifier passes and before the task is marked done, for tasks whose kit declared the `red-team` role. ([agentmc15/polymarket-trader](https://github.com/agentmc15/polymarket-trader/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/mm-proveout-red-team.md) · ★26)
- **mm-proveout-reviewer**: Dispatch mm-proveout-reviewer during /polytropos:execute mm-proveout at each phase boundary. ([agentmc15/polymarket-trader](https://github.com/agentmc15/polymarket-trader/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/mm-proveout-reviewer.md) · ★26)
- **mm-proveout-test-author**: Dispatch mm-proveout-test-author during /polytropos:execute mm-proveout after the implementer reports a task done and before the verifier runs, for tasks whose kit declared the `test-author` role. ([agentmc15/polymarket-trader](https://github.com/agentmc15/polymarket-trader/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/mm-proveout-test-author.md) · ★26)
- **mm-proveout-verifier**: Dispatch mm-proveout-verifier during /polytropos:execute mm-proveout after an implementer reports a task done. ([agentmc15/polymarket-trader](https://github.com/agentmc15/polymarket-trader/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/mm-proveout-verifier.md) · ★26)

- **mmp-creative-analytics**: Analyzes performance by creative across networks using MMP data (installs, events, revenue, ROAS per ad). - **mmp-cross-platform**: Measures owned media and other platforms: web-to-app (smart banners, Smart Script), email, SMS, QR codes, referrals, influencers, CTV, PC and console. ([anishdagdu/ai-marketing-agents](https://github.com/anishdagdu/ai-marketing-agents/blob/HEAD/mmp-agents/agents/mmp-cross-platform.md) · ★1)

- **mmp-in-app-events**: Designs and audits in-app event tracking: event taxonomy, revenue events, subscriptions, receipt validation and uninstall measurement. - **mnemosyne-researcher**: Epistemological and technical fact-checking subagent. ([zatzk/mnemosyne](https://github.com/zatzk/mnemosyne/blob/HEAD/agents/mnemosyne-researcher.md))
- **Mnemosyne-subagent.agent**: Setup and troubleshooting specialist for Supabase, BullMQ, and Redis ([MikeBlakeway/tribes](https://github.com/MikeBlakeway/tribes/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/Mnemosyne-subagent.agent.md))
- **mnemosyne-svg-maker**: Generates clean, deterministic custom SVGs for spatial geometry, coordinate plots, vectors, and contiguous memory/byte layouts to `/tmp/viz-output.svg`, verifies rendering (up to 3 iterations), and registers via `python3 scripts/mnemosyne.py artifact set --topic &lt;T&gt; --node &lt;N&gt; --file /tmp/viz-output.svg`. ([zatzk/mnemosyne](https://github.com/zatzk/mnemosyne/blob/HEAD/agents/mnemosyne-svg-maker.md))
- **mnm-architect**: Spécialiste architecture MnM. À utiliser quand on conçoit une nouvelle feature touchant à plusieurs services (backend + UI + MCP), à la sécurité multi-tenant, à la trace pipeline, aux config layers, ou à l'orchestration (Governed Workflows, CAO). Connaît le decision-log, les middlewares, et les compromis architecturaux passés. À utiliser AVANT d'écrire du code, pour designer puis valider une approche. ([AlphaLuppi/mnm](https://github.com/AlphaLuppi/mnm/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/mnm-architect.md) · ★2)
- **mnm-backend**: Spécialiste backend MnM (Express + PostgreSQL + Drizzle + 71 services). À utiliser pour toute modification du serveur : nouvelle route API, nouveau service, migration Drizzle, fix RLS, debugging multi-tenant, optimisation query, intégration MCP server, SSE handler, WebSocket. Connaît la middleware chain et les patterns RLS du projet. ([AlphaLuppi/mnm](https://github.com/AlphaLuppi/mnm/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/mnm-backend.md) · ★2)
- **mnm-frontend**: Spécialiste frontend MnM (React 18 + shadcn/ui + Tailwind + Vite). À utiliser pour toute modification UI : nouvelle page, nouveau composant, intégration SSE/WebSocket, hooks data fetching, gestion état, mise à jour parity tracker, intégration AI Assistant, Workflow Studio. Connaît les patterns shadcn et la règle no-polling. ([AlphaLuppi/mnm](https://github.com/AlphaLuppi/mnm/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/mnm-frontend.md) · ★2)
- **mnn-source-inspector**: Use this agent to investigate Alibaba MNN internals from the actual source — architecture, public API signatures and ownership semantics, implementation details (sessions, tensors, LLM engine, kernel/backend dispatch) and the calling repository's MNN patches — in any repository that builds on or converts to MNN (e.g. SpeechLM.cpp.v3, Granite, Qwen2.5-Omni.cpp).
